- `3.1` Nif. same. Ex. R. l. c. end שלא יִנָּזְקוּ that they be not hurt (by the wolves). Deut. R. s. 7 ולא תִנָּזֵק צפורנו של אחד מהם rather than a nail of one of them be injured; a. e.—Usu. part. נִיזּוֹק, נִזּוֹק; pl. נִיזּוֹקִים, נִיזּוֹקִין. Ber. 9ᵇ אינו נ׳ כל וכ׳ has…

### `jastrow:נָחָשׁ` נָחָשׁ (m.): [the hissing,] serpent. Gen. R. s. 22 נ׳ הקדמוני the original serpent (the seducer of Eve). Gen. R. s. 20 נ׳ זה רשע בעל תשובות that serpent is wicked and skilled in arguments. Bekh. 8ᵃ נ׳ לשבע שנים וכ׳ a serpent’s pregnancy lasts seven years, and for that wick…
- `1` [the hissing,] serpent. Gen. R. s. 22 נ׳ הקדמוני the original serpent (the seducer of Eve). Gen. R. s. 20 נ׳ זה רשע בעל תשובות that serpent is wicked and skilled in arguments. Bekh. 8ᵃ נ׳ לשבע שנים וכ׳ a serpent’s pregnancy lasts seven years, and for that wick…
- `2` a pungent (poisonous) fluid in the leaves or in the stems of onions kept for a long time in the ground. Erub. 29ᵇ
- `3` a disease of the eye, v. חִלָּזוֹן. Bekh. VI, 2 חלזון נ׳ וכ׳, expl. ib. 38ᵇ as identical with חלזון; Tosef. ib. IV, 2; Sifra Emor ch. II, Par. 3.

### `jastrow:נָפַל` נָפַל: (b. h.) to fall, lie down; to be dropped; to occur. Sabb. XVI, 2 נָפְלָה דליקה if a conflagration takes place. Pes. II, 3, v. מַפּוֹלֶת. Y. Sot. VII, 21ᵈ top (ref. to יקים, Deut. XXVII, 26) וכי יש תורה נוֹפֶלֶת is there a falling law (to need erection)? Ned. 6…
- `1` (b. h.) to fall, lie down; to be dropped; to occur. Sabb. XVI, 2 נָפְלָה דליקה if a conflagration takes place. Pes. II, 3, v. מַפּוֹלֶת. Y. Sot. VII, 21ᵈ top (ref. to יקים, Deut. XXVII, 26) וכי יש תורה נוֹפֶלֶת is there a falling law (to need erection)? Ned. 6…
- `2.1` Hif. to cause to fall; to throw down. Gen. R. s. 26, v. נָפִיל. B. Kam. III, 10 ה׳ את שינו struck out his tooth; Tosef. ib. IX, 23; a. fr.—Esp. to miscarry. Nidd. III, 1 המַפֶּלֶת וכ׳ if a woman loses a lump-shaped embryo; a. fr.
- `3.1` Hithpa. to prostrate one’s self. Deut. R. s. 2, v. נִיפּוּל.
- `4.1` Nif. (denom. of נִיפּוּל) to fall apart, be spoiled. Tosef. Sabb. VI (VII), 14 שלא תִינָּפֵל הפת, v. צָוַוח.

### `jastrow:נָצַח` נָצַח: [to be bright, pure,] (cmp. זָכָה) to be victorious, win, prevail. Y. Sabb. VII, 5ᵇ top אם לִנְצוֹחַ אם להִינָּצֵחַ whether to conquer or to be conquered. Pes. 119ᵃ (play on לַמְנַצֵּחַ) זמרו למי שנוֹצְחִין אותו ושמח sing to him who rejoices when they conquer …
- `1` [to be bright, pure,] (cmp. זָכָה) to be victorious, win, prevail. Y. Sabb. VII, 5ᵇ top אם לִנְצוֹחַ אם להִינָּצֵחַ whether to conquer or to be conquered. Pes. 119ᵃ (play on לַמְנַצֵּחַ) זמרו למי שנוֹצְחִין אותו ושמח sing to him who rejoices when they conquer …
- `2.1` Pi. to make illustrious, to glorify. Midr. Till. l. c. (expl. למנצח) למי שהוא נאה לנַצֵּחַ to him whom it is befitting to glorify.
- `2.2` Pi. to conquer, prevail over. Ib. מלך ב"וד מְנַצְּחִים אותו וכ׳ a human king is angry when people defeat him (in argument; cmp. זְכֵי Pa.); Pes. l. c.—B. Mets. 59ᵇ נִצְּחוּנִי בני (or נְצָחוּנִי) my children have won over me. Ib. תלמידי חכמים שמנצחים וכ׳ scholars …
- `3.1` Nif. to be defeated. Y. Sabb. II, 5ᵇ top v. supra.

### `jastrow:נָתַן` נָתַן: (b. h.) to give; to place, put. Gitt. I, 6 תֵּן גט וכ׳ give this letter of divorce to my wife. Ib. תְּנוּ גט וכ׳ give ye &c. Ib. לא יִתְּנוּ לאחר מיתה they must not deliver it after the man’s death; a. v. fr.—נשא ונ׳, v. נָשָׂא.—נ׳ עיניו ל (to put an eye upon)…
- `1` (b. h.) to give; to place, put. Gitt. I, 6 תֵּן גט וכ׳ give this letter of divorce to my wife. Ib. תְּנוּ גט וכ׳ give ye &c. Ib. לא יִתְּנוּ לאחר מיתה they must not deliver it after the man’s death; a. v. fr.—נשא ונ׳, v. נָשָׂא.—נ׳ עיניו ל (to put an eye upon)…
- `2.1` Nif. to be given, put &c.; to be intended. Ned. 38ᵃ לא נִיתְּנָה תורה אלא וכ׳ the Law was given only to Moses and his descendants. Ib. וכולן נִתְּנוּ וכ׳ and all those (gates of understanding) were granted to Moses; a. fr.—Keth. 81ᵃ לא ניתנה כתובה וכ׳ the K’thubah …
- `3.1` Hof. to be put. Makhsh. I, 1 בכי יוּתַּן (הרי זה) it comes under the law (Lev. XI, 38), i.e. it is a liquid which, if put on eatables, makes them susceptible of uncleanness. Ib. 2 אינן בכי יותן do not qualify for uncleanness; a. fr.

### `jastrow:סְרַח ²` סְרַח ²: , to hang over. Targ. O. Ex. XXVI, 12. Part. סְרִיחַ. Ib. 13.
- `1` , to hang over. Targ. O. Ex. XXVI, 12. Part. סְרִיחַ. Ib. 13.
- `2.1` Pa. to cause to hang over. Part. pass. מְסָרַח. B. Kam. 117ᵃ ומְסָרְחֵי וכ׳, v. גְּבִינָא I.

### `jastrow:סָרַח I` סָרַח I: (Saf. of ארח; cmp. סרג, סרך) to entangle, intercept. Y. Succ. V, 55ᶜ bot. מפני שהוא סוֹרֵחַ את הנעימה because it (the sound of the organ) intercepts (confounds) the melody of the song; (Arakh. 10ᵇ מערבב)—Esp. (of a rough-edged surface) to catch (cmp. סִכְסֵךְ)…
- `1` (Saf. of ארח; cmp. סרג, סרך) to entangle, intercept. Y. Succ. V, 55ᶜ bot. מפני שהוא סוֹרֵחַ את הנעימה because it (the sound of the organ) intercepts (confounds) the melody of the song; (Arakh. 10ᵇ מערבב)—Esp. (of a rough-edged surface) to catch (cmp. סִכְסֵךְ)…
- `2.1` Hif. same, to lacerate. Keth. 61ᵇ שפשתן מַסְרִיחַ את הפה the flax (which the spinner moistens with saliva) makes the mouth sore; Y. ib. V, 30ᵃ bot. מַסְרַחַת, v. שִׁלְבֵּק.
- `3.1` Pi. to clutch, grasp. Erub. 54ᵃ (ref. to Ps. LXVIII, 11) אם אדם משים … שמְסָרֶחֶת ואוכלת וכ׳ if one makes himself like a beast of prey which seizes (with its claws) and eats (differ. in comment.), his learning will stay with him, i.e. only he who is plain in his l…

### `jastrow:סָרַח II` סָרַח II: Saf. of רוח, cmp. רָוַח; cmp. Arab. saraḥa) to be or to make wide; to extend.—Part. pass. סָרוּחַ; f. סְרוּחָה; pl. סְרוּחִים, סְרוּחִין; סְרוּחות a) overhanging, flapping. Yoma 83ᵇ ואזניו ס׳ and his (the mad dog’s) ears flap.—b) (v. Am. VI, 4; 7) stretched in…
- `1` Saf. of רוח, cmp. רָוַח; cmp. Arab. saraḥa) to be or to make wide; to extend.—Part. pass. סָרוּחַ; f. סְרוּחָה; pl. סְרוּחִים, סְרוּחִין; סְרוּחות a) overhanging, flapping. Yoma 83ᵇ ואזניו ס׳ and his (the mad dog’s) ears flap.—b) (v. Am. VI, 4; 7) stretched in…

### `jastrow:סָרַח III` סָרַח III: (v. precd.; cmp. רִיחַ) to evaporate, be decomposed; to decay; to smell badly.—Part. pass. סָרוּחַ; f. סְרוּחָה; pl. סְרוּחִים, סְרוּחִין; סְרוּחוֹת. Ter. III, 1; a. fr.—Trnsf. a) (cmp. חָמַץ) to sin, offend. Snh. 71ᵇ; Keth. 45ᵃ סָרְחָה ולבסוף בגרה if she sinn…
- `1` (v. precd.; cmp. רִיחַ) to evaporate, be decomposed; to decay; to smell badly.—Part. pass. סָרוּחַ; f. סְרוּחָה; pl. סְרוּחִים, סְרוּחִין; סְרוּחוֹת. Ter. III, 1; a. fr.—Trnsf. a) (cmp. חָמַץ) to sin, offend. Snh. 71ᵇ; Keth. 45ᵃ סָרְחָה ולבסוף בגרה if she sinn…
- `2.1` Nif. to become vapid, be stupid. Ḥag. 5ᵇ (ref. to Jer. l. c.) כיון שאבדה … נִסְרַחַת וכ׳ when counsel was gone from the children (of Israel), the wisdom of the nations became vapid.
- `3.1` Hif. to make offensive. Sabb. 62ᵇ (ref. to סרחים, Am. VI, 4) מַסְרִיחִין וכ׳ make their beds offensive with effusion &c.; Kidd. 71ᵇ. Tanḥ. Vaëra 14 הִסְרִיחוּ מצרים they made Egypt stink; a. e.
- `3.2` Hif. to become vapid, putrid; to smell badly. Cant. R. to II, 13 היין יַסְרִיחַ the wine will become vapid (Sot. l. c., a. e. היין ביוקר). Gen. R. s. 34 הוא מַסְרִיחַ it becomes putrid; ואינה מַסְרַחַת without decaying. Tanḥ. l. c. ויַסְרִיחוּ במצרים and spread ste…

- `1` [present, confirming,] (cmp. סהד) witness, evidence. Ḥull. 10ᵇ, a. e. עד אחד נאמן וכ׳ in matters of ritual the statement of one witness is sufficient. Bekh. 36ᵃ, a. e. עד מפי עד a witness testifying to what he has heard from an eye-witness. R. Hash. 26, a. e. …

### `jastrow:עֵץ` עֵץ (m.): tree, pole; wood. Esth. R. to V, 12 לאחר שעשה העץ after having made the gallows. Tam. II, 3, a. fr. עץ שמן pine-wood. Gen. R. s. 15 אילן שעֵצוֹ נאכל וכ׳ a tree whose wood is as fit to eat as its fruit. Sifra Emor, Par. 12, ch. XVI, v. עָנָף; a. v. fr.—Pes. 30ᵇ…
- `1` tree, pole; wood. Esth. R. to V, 12 לאחר שעשה העץ after having made the gallows. Tam. II, 3, a. fr. עץ שמן pine-wood. Gen. R. s. 15 אילן שעֵצוֹ נאכל וכ׳ a tree whose wood is as fit to eat as its fruit. Sifra Emor, Par. 12, ch. XVI, v. עָנָף; a. v. fr.—Pes. 30ᵇ…

### `jastrow:עֶרֶב` עֶרֶב (m.): sunset, evening. Ber. I, 3, sq. בָּעֶ׳ in the evening (at prayer); a. v. fr.—Trnsf. decline. Gen. R. s. 50 בָּעֶרֶב בָּא עַרְבָהּ של סדום baʿereb (Gen. XIX, 1), the evening of Sodom has come. Esth. R. introd. מי יתן ערבה, v. בֹּקֶר; a. fr.
- `1` sunset, evening. Ber. I, 3, sq. בָּעֶ׳ in the evening (at prayer); a. v. fr.—Trnsf. decline. Gen. R. s. 50 בָּעֶרֶב בָּא עַרְבָהּ של סדום baʿereb (Gen. XIX, 1), the evening of Sodom has come. Esth. R. introd. מי יתן ערבה, v. בֹּקֶר; a. fr.
- `2` entrance, the day preceding a certain day, the year preceding a certain period, eve. Sabb. II, 7 ע׳ שבת (abbrev. ע"ש) on Friday. Pes. 99ᵇ ע׳ הפסח the eve of the Passover. Taan. IV, 7 ע׳ תשעה באב the day preceding the ninth of Ab. Shebi. I, 1 ע׳ שביעית the year…

### `jastrow:עַל` עַל (m.): height; (prepos.) upon, above; about &c.—With suff. (pl.) עָלַי, עָלֶיךָ &c. Ab. I, 2 על שלשה וכ׳ the world stands on three things. Sabb. II, 6 על שלש וכ׳ for three sins &c. Snh. 20ᵃ, a. fr. אמרו עָלָיו על וכ׳ they relate about &c. Nidd. 23ᵇ מי שלבו דוה עליו w…
- `1` height; (prepos.) upon, above; about &c.—With suff. (pl.) עָלַי, עָלֶיךָ &c. Ab. I, 2 על שלשה וכ׳ the world stands on three things. Sabb. II, 6 על שלש וכ׳ for three sins &c. Snh. 20ᵃ, a. fr. אמרו עָלָיו על וכ׳ they relate about &c. Nidd. 23ᵇ מי שלבו דוה עליו w…

- `1` Targ. Gen. I, 2; a. v. fr.—With suffix (pl.) עֲלַי, עֲלָךְ, עֲלוֹהִי &c. Ib. XXVII, 12; a. v. fr.—Ab. II, 6 על דאטפת, v. טוּף.—ייתי עֲלַי, v. אֲתָא; a. v. fr.

### `jastrow:עַמּוּד` עַמּוּד (m.): column, stand. Ex. R. s. 1, ע׳ השן an ivory column. Y. Ber. IX, 13ᵃ אדם … ועומד אחורי הע׳ וכ׳ a man enters a synagogue and stands behind a stand praying silently, and the Lord listens. Bab. Ber. 28ᵇ (of R. Joḥănan) ע׳ הימיני thou right-hand pillar (cmp. I King…
- `1` column, stand. Ex. R. s. 1, ע׳ השן an ivory column. Y. Ber. IX, 13ᵃ אדם … ועומד אחורי הע׳ וכ׳ a man enters a synagogue and stands behind a stand praying silently, and the Lord listens. Bab. Ber. 28ᵇ (of R. Joḥănan) ע׳ הימיני thou right-hand pillar (cmp. I King…
- `2` cylinder around which a scroll is rolled, handle. Yad. III, 4 אינו … עד שיעשה לו ע׳ does not make the hands unclean, until he fastens a handle to it. B. Bath. 13ᵇ כדי לגול ע׳ blank margin wide enough to roll around the cylinder. Ib. 14ᵃ ספר תורה … ועושה לו ע׳ …
- `3` ע׳ השחר the morning dawn. Ber. I, 1; a. fr.
- `4` the fecal mass in the rectum; also the jet of urine when being discharged. Ber. 62ᵇ; 25ᵃ ע׳ החוזד וכ׳ a suppressed discharge produces dropsy. Ib. כנגד ע׳ בלבד only in the sight of urine at its discharge; a. e.
- `5` (bot.) the central stalk of onions &c., the scape. Ukts. I, 2 הע׳ שהוא מכוון וכ׳ the scape as far as it is surrounded by the edible part, the scape within the bulb; ib. 3 הע׳ שאינו מכוון וכ׳ that part of the scape which protrudes above the bulb.—[B. Bath. IV, …

### `jastrow:עַמּוּד ²` עַמּוּד ²: column, stand. Targ. Y. Gen. XIX, 26. Targ. Ex. XIII, 22; a. fr.—עמוד קריצתא = h. עמוד השחר, v. preced. Targ. Y. Gen. XXXII, 25.—Keth. 17ᵃ איפסיק ע׳ דנורא וכ׳ a column of fire was interposed between him and the rest of the world; ib. 77ᵇ; a. fr.—Pl. עַמּוּדִין…
- `1` column, stand. Targ. Y. Gen. XIX, 26. Targ. Ex. XIII, 22; a. fr.—עמוד קריצתא = h. עמוד השחר, v. preced. Targ. Y. Gen. XXXII, 25.—Keth. 17ᵃ איפסיק ע׳ דנורא וכ׳ a column of fire was interposed between him and the rest of the world; ib. 77ᵇ; a. fr.—Pl. עַמּוּדִין…
- `2` continuous flow, jet. Ab. Zar. 73ᵃ צירצור קטן דלא נפיש עַמּוּדֵיה a small bottle the jet from which is not large; חבית דנפיש עַמּוּדָהּ (not עמודיה, v. Rabb. D. S. a. l. note 400) a jug whose jet is large.

### `jastrow:עַקְרָב` עַקְרָב: c. (b. h.; prob. fr. עקב, with anorg. ר; cmp. עַרְקוֹב)
- `1` c. (b. h.; prob. fr. עקב, with anorg. ר; cmp. עַרְקוֹב)
- `2` scorpion. Ab. V, 5 ולא הזיק נחש וע׳ וכ׳ nor did ever a serpent or a scorpion harm anybody in Jerusalem. Ib. II, 10, v. עֲקִיצָה. Sabb. 121ᵇ ע׳ שבחדייב the scorpion of Adiabene; Y. ib. XIV, beg. 14ᵇ. Num. R. s. 10 היין משול בע׳ (some ed. כע׳) wine is compared t…
- `3` the constellation Scorpio. Pesik. R. s. 20. Num. R. l. c. כל שעה … אין ע׳ וכ׳ as long as Kimah (v. כִּימָה) is visible, Scorpio is invisible; a. fr.
- `4` bit of a bridle. Kel. XI, 5.
- `5` עַקְרַב בית הבד the iron-shod part of the pressbeam. Ib. XII, 3.

### `jastrow:עָל` עָל: verb, v. עוּל, עֲלַל.
- `1` verb, v. עוּל, עֲלַל.

- `3` to reduce to one-tenth. Cant. R. to VII, 11, v. עָשַׁר.

- `2.2` Hif. (sub. עצמו) to show one’s self obedient, be persuaded, surrender, v. Nif.
- `3.1` Pi. to announce, v. supra. Y. Shek. I, beg. 45ᶜ; Y. Meg. I, 71ᵃ.
- `3.2` Pi. to assemble for mourning services, invite. Snh. 47ᵃ הלינו לכבודו לשַׁמֵּעַ עליו וכ׳ if burial is postponed for the honor of the dead, to assemble the surrounding townships for his funeral, to bring on lamenting women &c.
- `4.1` Nif. to be heard; to be listened to, be obeyed. Ber. 6ᵃ אין תפלה … נִשְׁמַעַת וכ׳ man’s prayer is heard only in the house of prayer. Y. ib. V, end, 9ᵇ שׁנִשְׁמְעָה תפלתו that his prayer will be heard (his petition be granted). Erub. 18ᵇ כל בית שׁנִשְׁמָעִין וכ׳ a h…
- `4.2` Nif. to show one’s self willing; to submit to authority; (of woman) to surrender. R. Hash. 25ᵇ אשרי הדור שהגדולים נִשְׁמָעִים וכ׳ blessed the generation in which the superiors (in wisdom) submit to the authority of their inferiors, and how much more when inferiors …
